- **enumerate:** decide a LET operator's call-by-name route once per definition; JS1 no longer open

- **compiler:** memoize bounded materialized-set DAG analysis
- **compiler:** build bounded filters with one bulk constructor
- **compiler:** construct bounded range functions once

- **enumerate:** the enumerated-branch replay skip is opt-in (TY\_OPT\_IN=enumerated-branch-replay-skip), default OFF
- **const-set cache:** record a held definition's node by address, with the definition pinned
- **const-set cache:** an entry records a structural digest and copies its node at the first hit
- **enabled:** take the partial-successor context only for a right side that may read a prime
- **enabled:** decide once per ENABLED evaluation whether any prime is read off an assignment target
- **enabled:** cache the once-per-evaluation partial-successor decision per action
- **fused:** a fused run the tiny-graph proof decided reads its SMT-compatibility report from the BFS checker's setup instead of loading every module again
- **mixed-value-tlc-parity:** repair round 1 — a membership probe of a set marked as normalized in place costs one load and an inline scalar test; MC\_4 back inside the gate ratchet
- **w10:** release the lowered module once the thread's kernel is compiled (PROTOCOL §11 item 1: median 4.7 MB, 107 MB at 193 transitions of live heap held by the process cache); the process cache keeps refusals only (TrustCG refusals now too); prereg §13
- **mixed-value-tlc-parity:** an equal successor set is decided by its cached TLC shape before the materialization walk (DR-119)
- **tla-petri/ltl-scc:** intern successor markings lazily, marked-place enabled scan, resident guard band, release freed pages around each search
- **mixed-value-tlc-parity:** the set compare reads the raw order without the shared normalization cache, and only for a raw, uncertified set (DR-119)
- **mixed-value-tlc-parity:** a set of scalars, or of tuples of scalars, takes its TLC shape without interning one per element
- **fused-deep-filter-stack:** the next-mode demand walk looks for a set of names at once, so a LET chain is linear per name (DR-123)
- **fused-deep-filter-stack:** the bytecode compiler declines a LET nested deeper than 256 before its per-LET analyses, TY\_LEGACY=bytecode-deep-let compiles at any depth (DR-123)
- **fused-deep-filter-stack:** layout inference's nested-writer call scan clones no actuals the caller skips, so Inc(Inc(...)) is quadratic, not cubic (DR-123)
- **fused-deep-filter-stack:** operator expansion resolves a LET name through an index, so an N-deep LET chain is no longer O(N^3) to expand (DR-123)
- **fused-deep-filter-stack:** Spanned's PartialEq and Hash drop the stack guard, and its Clone guards only Spanned\<Expr\> (DR-123)
- **fused-deep-filter-stack:** the stack guard's fast path reads the stack pointer inline and its limit without a lazy check; a leaf's clone and a shallow LET nest skip the guard's and the index's costs (DR-123)
- **fused-deep-filter-stack:** folds, visitors, translation and TIR lowering skip the stack guard at a leaf (DR-123)
- **init-tuple-pattern:** bind each product coordinate per binding (one fold, groups share their elements), not every element's expressions up front (g04 RSS 158 MB -\> 211 MB with them)
- **init-tuple-pattern:** rebind only the product coordinates whose element moved (coordinate 0 per binding, the others at a carry)
- **standalone-instance-action:** skip the substituted-origin scan when no unnamed INSTANCE WITH exists
- **value:** borrow clone-stable single-leaf sequence views
- **state:** reuse exclusive compact value boxes on replacement
- **state:** retain parent backing for empty owning diffs
- **mcc:** reuse immutable guided walk setup across residual batches
